. Also, points (1), (2), and (3) of theorem
1.3
hold. Therefore, we have finished the proof of Theorem
1.3
.

∎

All that remains is to prove Proposition
8.1
. We will postpone this proof until Section
8.3
. Instead, we turn to introduce Proposition
8.2
, which, compared to Proposition
8.1
, provides a small-data existence result with a smoother vorticity.

8.2.
Proposition
8.2
for small data

By the finite propagation speed of system (
1.22
), we denote
c>0
as the largest speed.

Proposition 8.2
.

Assume
s\in(\frac{7}{4},2],\delta\in(0,s-\frac{7}{4})
and (
1.13
) hold. Let
\delta_{1}=\frac{s-\frac{7}{4}}{10}
. For each small, smooth initial data
(\mathbf{v}_{0},\rho_{0},w_{0})
supported in
B(0,c+2)
which satisfies

\begin{split}&\|\mathbf{v}_{0}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\rho_{0}\|_{H^{s}}+\|w_{0}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla w_{0}\|_{L^{8}}\leq\epsilon_{3},\end{split}

(8.15)

there exists a smooth solution
(\mathbf{v},\rho,w)
to (
1.22
) on
[-2,2]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying

\|\mathbf{v}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\rho\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|w\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla w\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}L_{x}^{8}}\leq\epsilon_{2}.

(8.16)

Furthermore, the solution satisfies the following properties

(1)

dispersive estimate for
\mathbf{v}
,
\rho
, and
\mathbf{v}_{+}

\|d\mathbf{v},d\rho,d\mathbf{v}_{+}\|_{L^{4}_{[-2,2]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq\epsilon_{2}.

(8.17)

(2)

For any
t_{0}\in[-2,2]
, let
f
satisfy equation

\begin{cases}&\square_{g}f=0,\\
&(f,\partial_{t}f)|_{t=t_{0}}=(f_{0},f_{1}).\end{cases}

(8.18)

For each
1\leq r\leq s+1
, the Cauchy problem (
8.18
) is well-posed in
H_{x}^{r}\times H_{x}^{r-1}
, and the following estimate holds:

\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a}f\|_{L^{4}_{[-2,2]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\lesssim\|f_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}},\quad\ a<r-\frac{3}{4},

(8.19)

and the same estimates hold if we replace
\left<\nabla\right>^{a}
by
\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}d
.

Proof.

For
\frac{7}{4}+\delta_{1}<s
, by replacing the regularity exponents in Proposition
3.2
to
s=s,s_{0}=\frac{7}{4}+\delta_{1}
, the conclusion of Proposition
8.2
follows immediately.
∎

Remark 8.1
.

This Strichartz estimates is stronger than (
8.9
), since the regularity of vorticity in Proposition
8.2
is higher than that in Proposition
8.1
.

8.3.
Proof of Proposition
8.1

To prove Proposition
8.1
, we divide the proof into three parts. First, as presented in subsection
8.3.1
, we obtain a solution to Proposition
8.1
over a short time interval. Second, we derive robust Strichartz estimates for the low- and mid-frequency components of the solutions within these short intervals, albeit with a loss of derivatives, as demonstrated in subsection
8.3.2
. Finally, in subsection
8.3.3
, we extend these solutions to a regular time interval. Similarly, the solutions to the linear wave equations can also be extended to this regular time interval, as shown in subsection
8.3.4
.

8.3.1.
Energy estimates and Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

Take the scaling

\begin{split}&\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}={\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(Tt,Tx),\quad\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}=\rho_{0j}(Tt,Tx).\end{split}

Referring to (
1.18
), we define

\widetilde{w}_{0j}=\mathrm{e}^{-\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}.

Thus, we can infer

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}\leq&T^{s-1}\|(\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}),\\
\|\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}=&\|\mathrm{e}^{-\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}\\
\leq&T^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{1}}\|\mathrm{e}^{-{\rho}_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{2+\delta_{1}}}\\
=&T^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{1}}\|{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}\\
\leq&T^{\frac{1}{2}+\delta_{1}}2^{\delta_{1}j}\|{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}}}.\end{split}

(8.20)

Similarly, we can deduce

\begin{split}\|\nabla\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}\leq&T^{\frac{7}{4}}\|\nabla{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}.\end{split}

Define

T^{*}_{j}=2^{-\delta_{1}j}[1+E(0)]^{-3},

(8.21)

where
E(0)
is stated as in (
8.5
). Taking
T
in (
8.20
) as
T^{*}_{j}
, for
s>\frac{7}{4}
, (
8.20
) yields

\begin{split}&\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}\leq 2^{-\frac{\delta^{2}_{1}}{2}j}[1+E(0)]^{-\frac{1}{2}}.\end{split}

Note
\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}
and
\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|{\rho}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}
. Due to (
8.6
), we have

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}+\|{\rho}_{0j}\|_{L^{\infty}}\leq C_{0}.\end{split}

For a small parameter
\epsilon_{3}
stated in Proposition
8.2
, we choose
N_{0}=N_{0}(s,M_{0},c_{0},C_{0})
such that

\begin{split}&2^{-\frac{\delta^{2}_{1}}{2}N_{0}}[1+E(0)]^{-\frac{1}{2}}\ll\epsilon_{3},\\
&2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}(1+C_{*})^{3}\{1+{C^{3}_{*}}[E(0)]^{-3}[1+E(0)]^{-3}\}\leq 1,\\
&C2^{-\frac{3}{4}\delta_{1}N_{0}}[E(0)]^{-\frac{9}{4}}[1+E(0)]^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}\leq 2.\end{split}

(8.22)

Above,
C_{*}
is denoted by

\begin{split}C_{*}=CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\exp\{CE(0)\textrm{e}^{2}\}.\end{split}

(8.23)

Therefore, for
j\geq N_{0}
, we have

\begin{split}\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}\leq\epsilon_{3}.\end{split}

(8.24)

Since the estimate (
8.24
) is formulated in terms of homogeneous norms, we need to use physical localization techniques to extend these bounds to the inhomogeneous case.

Define a smooth function
\chi\in C_{0}^{\infty}(\mathbb{R}^{2})
supported in
B(0,c+2)
, and
\chi
equals
1
in
B(0,c+1)
. Given
y\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we define the localized initial data for the velocity and density near

:

\begin{split}\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(x)=&\chi(x-y)\left(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(x)-\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y)\right),\\
\bar{\rho}_{0j}(x)=&\chi(x-y)\left(\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(x)-\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)\right).\end{split}

(8.25)

Referring to (
1.18
), we set

\bar{w}_{0j}=\mathrm{e}^{-\bar{\rho}_{0j}}\mathrm{curl}\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}.

(8.26)

Taking advantage of (
8.24
), (
8.25
) and (
8.26
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{{H}^{s}}+\|\bar{\rho}_{0j}\|_{{H}^{s}}+\|\bar{w}_{0j}\|_{{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla\bar{w}_{0j}\|_{{L}^{8}}\\
\leq&C(\|\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}+\|\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla\widetilde{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}})\\
\leq&\epsilon_{3}.\end{split}

(8.27)

Using Proposition
8.2
, for every
j
, there is a smooth solution
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j},\bar{\rho}_{j},\bar{w}_{j})
on
[-2,2]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying

\begin{cases}\square_{\widetilde{g}_{j}}\bar{v}_{j}^{i}=-\epsilon^{ia}\mathrm{e}^{\bar{\rho}_{j}+\tilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)}\widetilde{c}_{j}^{2}\partial_{a}\bar{w}_{j}+\widetilde{{Q}}^{i},\\
\square_{\widetilde{g}_{j}}\bar{\rho}_{j}=\widetilde{\mathcal{D}},\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{T}}\bar{w}_{j}=0,\\
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j},\bar{\rho}_{j},\bar{w}_{j})|_{t=0}=(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j},\bar{\rho}_{0j},\bar{w}_{0j}).\end{cases}

(8.28)

Above, the quantities
\widetilde{c}^{2}_{j}
and
\widetilde{{g}_{j}}
are defined by

\begin{split}\widetilde{c}^{2}_{j}&=c^{2}_{s}(\bar{\rho}_{j}+\tilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)),\\
\widetilde{g}_{j}&=g({\bar{\mathbf{v}}}_{j}+\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y),{\bar{\rho}}_{j}+\tilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)),\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{T}}&=\partial_{t}+({\bar{\mathbf{v}}}_{j}+\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y))\cdot\nabla,\end{split}

(8.29)

and
\widetilde{{Q}}^{i},i=1,2
and
\widetilde{\mathcal{D}}
have the same formulations with
{Q}^{i},\mathcal{D}
by replacing
({\mathbf{v}},{\rho})
to
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\tilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0}(y),\bar{\rho}_{j}+\tilde{\rho}_{0}(y))
. Using Proposition
8.2
again, on
[-2,2]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we infer

\|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\bar{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{s}}+\|\bar{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}+\delta_{1}}}+\|\nabla\bar{w}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[-2,2]}L_{x}^{8}}\leq\epsilon_{2},

and

\|d\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d\bar{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[-2,2]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq\epsilon_{2}.

Furthermore, using (
8.19
), for
1\leq r\leq s+1
, the linear equation
5
5

5

Here
\widetilde{g}_{j}
is given by (
8.29
).

\begin{cases}&\square_{\widetilde{g}_{j}}F=0,\qquad\qquad\ \ \qquad[-2,2]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&({F},\partial_{t}{F})|_{t=t_{0}}=({F}_{0},{F}_{1}),\ \ \ t_{0}\in[-2,2],\end{cases}

(8.30)

admits a solution
{F}\in C([-2,2],H_{x}^{r})\times C^{1}([-2,2],H_{x}^{r-1})
. Moreover, for
k<r-1
, the following estimate holds
6
6

6

For all
j
, the initial norm of the initial data (
8.27
) is uniformly controlled by the same small parameter
\epsilon_{3}
, and the regularity of the initial data (
8.27
) only depends on
s
, so the constant in (
8.31
) is uniform for all
\widetilde{{g}}
depending on
j
.
:

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{k}F,\left<\nabla\right>^{k-1}d{F}\|_{L^{4}_{[-2,2]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|{F}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{F}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(8.31)

Note (
8.28
). Then the function
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y),\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y),\bar{w}_{j})
is also a solution of the following system

\begin{cases}\square_{\widetilde{g}_{j}}(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y))=-\epsilon^{ia}\mathrm{e}^{\bar{\rho}_{j}+\tilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)}\widetilde{c}_{s}^{2}\partial_{a}\bar{w}_{j}+\widetilde{{Q}}^{i},\\
\square_{\widetilde{g}_{j}}(\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y))=\widetilde{\mathcal{D}},\\
\widetilde{\mathbf{T}}\bar{w}_{j}=0,\\
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y),\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y),\bar{w}_{j})|_{t=0}=(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j},\widetilde{\rho}_{0j},\widetilde{w}_{0j}).\end{cases}

For
y\in\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we consider the restrictions

\left(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y)\right)|_{\mathrm{K}^{y}},\quad(\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y))|_{\mathrm{K}^{y}},\\
\quad\bar{w}_{j}|_{\mathrm{K}^{y}},

(8.32)

where

\mathrm{K}^{y}=\left\{(t,x):ct+|x-y|\leq c+1,|t|<1\right\}.

Then the restrictions (
8.32
) solve (
1.22
) on
\mathrm{K}^{y}
. Due to finite speed of propagation, a smooth solution
(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y),\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y),\bar{\mathbf{w}}_{j})
solves (
1.22
) on
\mathrm{K}^{y}
.

Let a function
\psi
be supported in the unit ball such that

\textstyle{\sum}_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{2}}\psi(x-y)=1.

Therefore, the function

\begin{split}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j}(t,x)&=\textstyle{\sum}_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{2}}\psi(x-y)(\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j}+\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}(y)),\\
\widetilde{\rho}_{j}(t,x)&=\textstyle{\sum}_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{2}}\psi(x-y)(\bar{\rho}_{j}+\widetilde{\rho}_{0j}(y)),\\
\widetilde{w}_{j}(t,x)&=\mathrm{e}^{-\widetilde{\rho}_{j}}\mathrm{curl}\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j},\end{split}

(8.33)

is a smooth solution of (
1.22
) on
[-1,1]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
with the initial data
(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j},\widetilde{\rho}_{j},\widetilde{w}_{j})|_{t=0}=(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j},\widetilde{\rho}_{0j},\widetilde{w}_{0j})
,

On the other hand, the initial data
(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{0j},\widetilde{\rho}_{0j},\widetilde{w}_{0j})
is a scaling of
(\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j},w_{0j})
with the space-time scale
T^{*}_{j}
, and the system (
1.22
) is scaling-invariant. Therefore, the function

({\mathbf{v}}_{j},{\rho}_{j},{w}_{j})=(\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j},\widetilde{\rho}_{j},\widetilde{w}_{j})((T^{*}_{j})^{-1}t,(T^{*}_{j})^{-1}x),

is a solution of (
1.22
) on
[0,T^{*}_{j}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
(
T^{*}_{j}
is defined as in (
8.21
)) with the initial data

({\mathbf{v}}_{j},{\rho}_{j},{w}_{j})|_{t=0}=({\mathbf{v}}_{0j},{\rho}_{0j},{w}_{0j}).

Referring (
8.33
) and using (
8.30
)-(
8.31
), we can see

\begin{split}\|d\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d\widetilde{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,1]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&\sup_{y\in 3^{-\frac{1}{2}}\mathbb{Z}^{2}}\|d\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d\bar{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,1]}{C^{\delta}_{x}}}\\
\leq&C(\|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\bar{\rho}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|\bar{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\nabla\bar{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}).\end{split}

(8.34)

By changing of coordinates
(t,x)\rightarrow((T_{j}^{*})^{-1}t,(T_{j}^{*})^{-1}x)
for each
j\geq 1
, we get

\begin{split}\|d{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{3}{4}+{\delta})}\|d\widetilde{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d\widetilde{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,1]}C^{\delta}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.35)

Using (
8.34
), (
8.35
), (
8.21
), and
{\delta}\in(0,s-\frac{7}{4})
, it follows

\begin{split}\|d{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&C(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{3}{4}+{\delta})}(\|\bar{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\bar{\rho}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{s}}+\|\bar{w}_{0j}\|_{H_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}})\\
\leq&C(T^{*}_{j})^{-(\frac{3}{4}+{\delta})}\left\{(T^{*}_{j})^{s-1}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0j},{\rho}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{s}}+(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{3}{2}}\|{w}_{0j}\|_{\dot{H}_{x}^{\frac{3}{2}}}+(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{7}{4}}\|\nabla{w}_{0j}\|_{L_{x}^{8}}\right\}\\
\leq&C(\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|{\rho}_{0j}\|_{H^{s}}+\|{w}_{0j}\|_{H^{\frac{3}{2}}}+\|\nabla{w}_{0j}\|_{L^{8}}).\end{split}

(8.36)

Comining (
8.36
) and (
8.5
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|d{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq&CE(0).\end{split}

(8.37)

Set

E(T^{*}_{j})=\|\mathbf{v}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{s}_{x}}+\|\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{s}_{x}}+\|w_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{\frac{3}{2}}_{x}}+\|\nabla w_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{8}_{x}}.

Due to (
8.37
), (
8.22
), and (
8.21
), we get

\begin{split}\|d{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq(T_{j}^{*})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|d{\mathbf{v}}_{j},d{\rho}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{\delta}_{x}}\leq 2.\end{split}

By using Theorem
2.10
, we have

\displaystyle E(T^{*}_{j})\leq CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\exp\{CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\}.

Similarly, for
1\leq r\leq s+1
, there exists a unique solution for the Cauchy problem

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}F=0,\quad(0,T^{*}_{j}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(F,\partial_{t}F)|_{t=0}=(F_{0},F_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}.\end{cases}

(8.38)

Moreover, for
a<r-1
, we have

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}dF\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|{F}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{F}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\end{split}

(8.39)

and

\begin{split}\|{F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{F}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T_{j}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq C(\|{F}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{F}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

Gathering the results above, there is a uniform bound for
\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}
and
w_{j}
. However, the length of the interval
[0,T_{j}^{*}]
is not uniform and depends on
j
. To construct a solution as the limit of these smooth solutions, we must extend them over a uniformly regular time interval.

8.3.2.
A loss of Strichartz estimates on a short time-interval.

We will discuss it into two cases
7
7

7

Here, we mainly inspired by Tataru
[
55
]
, Bahouri-Chemin
[
9
]
, and Ai-Ifrim-Tataru
[
2
]
. Of course, our work based on the property of Strichartz estimates in Proposition
6.2
and careful analysis on vorticity. Moreover, we eventually conclude it by induction method.
, the high frequency and low frequency for
d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}
and
d{\rho}_{j}
.

Case 1: high frequency(
k\geq j
).
Due to (
8.37
), we get

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\leq CE(0),\quad a\in[0,s-\frac{7}{4}).\end{split}

(8.40)

For
k\geq j
, using Bernstein inequality and (
8.40
), we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&2^{-ka}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}C^{a}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-ja}E(0).\end{split}

(8.41)

Taking
a=9\delta_{1}
in (
8.41
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-7\delta_{1}j},\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(8.42)

Case 2: low frequency(
k<j
).
In this case, it’s much different from the high frequency. Fortunately, there is some good estimates for difference terms
P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})
. Following (
1.20
) and (
1.21
), we set

\begin{split}&\mathbf{v}_{m}=\mathbf{v}_{+m}+\mathbf{v}_{-m},\quad\mathbf{v}_{\pm m}=(v^{1}_{\pm m},v^{2}_{\pm m}),\\
&-\Delta v^{i}_{-m}=\epsilon^{ia}\mathrm{e}^{\rho_{m}}\partial_{a}w_{m}.\end{split}

We will obtain some good estimates of
P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})
and
P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m})
by using (
8.38
)-(
8.39
). Indeed, this good estimate is from a loss of derivatives of Strichartz estimate. We claim that

\displaystyle\|\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-\mathbf{v}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0),

(8.43)

\displaystyle\|w_{m+1}-w_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(\frac{3}{2}-\delta_{1})m}E(0).

(8.44)

To verify (
8.43
) and (
8.44
), we start from the initial data. Applying Bernstein’s inequality, we get

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim&2^{-sm}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}}.\end{split}

(8.45)

Similarly, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|{\rho}_{0(m+1)}-{\rho}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-sm}\|{\rho}_{0(m+1)}-{\rho}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{s}},\\
&\|{w}_{0(m+1)}-{w}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{-{\frac{3}{2}}m}\|{w}_{0(m+1)}-{w}_{0m}\|_{\dot{H}^{\frac{3}{2}}}.\end{split}

(8.46)

Based on (
8.45
) and (
8.46
), we can use the structure of the system (
1.22
) to prove (
8.43
)-(
8.44
). Let
{\mathbf{U}}_{m}=(\mathbf{v}_{m},p(\rho_{m}))^{\mathrm{T}}
. Then
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
satisfies

\begin{cases}&A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{t}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})+A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})\partial_{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})=\Pi_{m},\\
&({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m})|_{t=0}={\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m},\end{cases}

(8.47)

where

\Pi_{m}=-[A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{0}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{t}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}-[A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m+1})-A^{i}({\mathbf{U}}_{m})]\partial_{i}{\mathbf{U}}_{m}.

(8.48)

From (
8.48
), we can see

|\Pi_{m}|\lesssim|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|\cdot|d{\mathbf{U}}_{m}|.

Multiplying
{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}
on (
8.47
) and integrating it on
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we have

\frac{d}{dt}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim\|(d{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1},d{\mathbf{U}}_{m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}.

(8.49)

Integrating (
8.49
) on
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
, using Gronwall’s inequality and Strichartz estimate (
8.37
), it yields

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim&\|{\mathbf{U}}_{0(m+1)}-{\mathbf{U}}_{0m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}.\end{split}

Due to (
8.45
) and (
8.46
), we then have

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{U}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{U}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0).\end{split}

By using Lemma
2.5
, we show that

\begin{split}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\leq 2^{-(s-\delta_{1})m}E(0).\end{split}

Thus, the estimate (
8.43
) holds. To prove (
8.44
), we consider the transport equation of
{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}
:

\partial_{t}({w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m})+({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\cdot\nabla)({w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m})=-({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\cdot\nabla{w}_{m}.

(8.50)

Multiplying with
{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}
on (
8.50
) and integrating it on
[0,t]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
, we derive

\begin{split}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|{w}_{0(m+1)}-{w}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}_{x}}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla{w}_{m}\|_{L^{8}_{x}}d\tau\\
\leq&\|{w}_{0(m+1)}-{w}_{0m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|\nabla{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|^{2}_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau\\
&+C\int^{t}_{0}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\nabla{w}_{m}\|_{L^{8}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.51)

We also note that

\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{H^{\frac{1}{4}}_{x}}\lesssim\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|^{1-\frac{1}{4s}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|^{\frac{1}{4s}}_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(8.52)

Taking advantage of (
8.46
), (
8.43
), (
8.40
), (
8.51
), and (
8.52
), and using Gronwall’s inequality gives

\begin{split}\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{2}_{x}}\lesssim 2^{-(\frac{3}{2}-\delta_{1})m}E(0).\end{split}

This implies that (
8.44
) holds. On the other hand, for
\delta_{1}<s-\frac{7}{4}
, using Strichartz estimates (
8.39
)(similar to (
6.12
)), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m},{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{7}{4}+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{1+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.53)

Noting
s=2+10\delta_{1}
and using (
8.43
)-(
8.44
), we can bound (
8.53
) by

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{+(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{+m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq 2^{-7\delta_{1}m}E(0).\end{split}

(8.54)

Applying (
8.44
) and Sobolev imbeddings, we can establish

\begin{split}\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq&\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{-(m+1)}-{\mathbf{v}}_{-m})\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{1+2\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}[1+E(0)]^{2}.\end{split}

(8.55)

Adding (
8.54
) and (
8.55
), it yields

\begin{split}&\|\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\leq C2^{-7\delta_{1}m}[1+E(0)]^{2}.\end{split}

(8.56)

By using (
1.22
) and (
8.56
), we obtain

\begin{split}&\|\partial_{t}({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{2}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|\nabla({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),\nabla({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}C^{\delta_{1}}_{x}}\cdot(1+\|\mathbf{v}_{m},\rho_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{s}_{x}})\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.57)

Due to (
8.56
) and (
8.57
), for
k<m
, we get

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot 2^{-6\delta_{1}m}[1+E(0)]^{3},

(8.58)

and

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}\cdot 2^{-6\delta_{1}m}[1+E(0)]^{3}.

8.3.3.
Uniform energy and Strichartz estimates on a regular time-interval
[0,T_{N_{0}}^{*}]
.

Recall (
8.21
), then
T_{N_{0}}^{*}=[E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}
. Recall (
8.42
) and (
8.58
). Therefore, when
k\geq j
and
j\geq N_{0}
, using (
8.42
) and Hölder’s inequality, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{j})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.59)

When
k<j
and
m\geq N_{0}
, using (
8.58
) and Hölder’s inequality, we obtain

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(8.60)

On the other side, when
k\geq j
and
j<N_{0}
, using (
8.42
), we get

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.61)

When
k<j
and
m<N_{0}
, using (
8.58
) and Hölder’s inequality, it yields

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{j+1}-{\rho}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{j+1}-{\rho}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{j+1}-{\rho}_{j}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{j+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{j})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.62)

Due to a different time-interval for the sequence
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
, we will discuss the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
or
j\geq N_{0}+1
as follows.

Case 1:
j\leq N_{0}
.
In this case,
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
exists on
[0,T_{j}^{*}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{j}]
. As a result, we don’t need to extend solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
if
j\leq N_{0}
. Using (
8.37
) and Hölder’s inequality, we get

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[1+E(0)].

By (
8.22
), this yields

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2.

By using Newton-Leibniz’s formula and (
8.6
), it follows that

\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{3}}}\leq|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0}.

Using Theorem
2.10
, we get

E(T^{*}_{N_{0}})\leq CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\exp\{CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\}.

Case 2:
j\geq N_{0}+1
.
In this case, we expect to extend the time interval
I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Our idea is to use (
8.42
), (
8.58
), and Theorem
2.10
to calculate the energy at time
T_{j}^{*}
. Starting at
T_{j}^{*}
, we can also get a new time-interval.

We set

I_{1}=[0,T^{*}_{j}]=[t_{0},t_{1}],\quad\quad|I_{1}|=[E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

By using frequency decomposition, we get

\begin{split}d\mathbf{v}_{j}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j}\\
=&P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k}.\end{split}

(8.63)

Similarly, we also have

\begin{split}d\rho_{j}=&P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}d\rho_{k}.\end{split}

(8.64)

When
k\geq j
, using (
8.59
) and (
8.61
), we have
8
8

8

In the case
j\geq N_{0}
we use (
8.59
). In the case
j<N_{0}
, we take
T^{*}_{j}=T^{*}_{N_{0}}
and use (
8.61
) to give a bound on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}.\end{split}

(8.65)

When
k<j
, using (
8.60
) and (
8.62
), it follows
9
9

9

In the case
m\geq N_{0}
we use (
8.60
). In the case

\|P_{k}d({\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}d({\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.

(8.66)

We will use and (
8.65
)-(
8.66
) to give a precise analysis of (
8.63
)-(
8.64
) and get some new time-intervals, and then we try to extend
\rho_{j}
from

to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Our strategy is as follows. In step 1, we extend it from

to

. In step 2, we use induction methods to conclude these estimates and also extend it to

.

Step 1: Extending
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]\ (j\geq N_{0}+1)
.
To start, referring (
8.21
), we need to calculate

for obtaining a length of a new time-interval. Then we shall calculate
\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}
. Using (
8.63
) and (
8.64
), we derive that

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

we get

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}\leq 2.\end{split}

(8.67)

By using (
8.67
) and (
8.6
), we get

\begin{split}\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq\|\mathbf{v}_{0j},\rho_{0j}\|+\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{0}+2.\end{split}

By (

), (

, we have

\begin{split}E(T^{*}_{j})\leq CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\exp\{CE(0)\mathrm{e}^{2}\}=C_{*}.\end{split}

(8.68)

Above,
C_{*}
is stated in (
8.23
). Starting at the time

, seeing (

) and (

Case 1:

T^{*}_{j}+(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}\geq T^{*}_{j-1}
. In this case, we can get a new interval

I_{2}=[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}],\quad|I_{2}|=(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)[E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}.

(8.69)

and
k<j
,

(8.71)

and

\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{j}-d{\rho}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{j}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j},T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\cdot C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}.

(8.72)

Therefore, we obtain the following estimate

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.73)

Due to (
8.65
) and (

\begin{split}\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-7\delta_{1}j}+2^{-6\delta_{1}j})\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2.\end{split}

(8.74)

Due to (
8.66
) and (

), it follows

\begin{split}&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{j}-d\mathbf{v}_{j-1}),P_{k}(d\rho_{j}-d\rho_{j-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}(2^{-6\delta_{1}(j-1)}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)})\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2.\end{split}

(8.75)

Inserting (
8.74
)-(
8.75
) into (

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}_{k=j}^{\infty}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}j}\times 2\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-2}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-2}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
\leq&C(1+E^{3}(0))(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(8.76)

By using (

), (
8.22

&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-1)}\times 2\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\

&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m},T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}m}+C[1+E(0)]^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(8.86)

For
k\geq m\geq N_{0}+1
, due to (
8.22
) and (
8.86
), it yields

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{m},P_{k}d\rho_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}},\end{split}

(8.87)

Similarly, if
m\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
8.22
), for
k<j
, the following estimate holds:

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m}-d{\rho}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m}-d{\rho}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m}-d{\rho}_{m-1}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m-1})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m},T^{*}_{m-1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(m-1)}\\
&+C(1+C_{*})^{3}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}(m-1)}\times(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}(m-1)}\times 2^{\delta_{1}}.\end{split}

(8.88)

Step 2: Extending time interval
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
. Based the above analysis in Step 1, we can give a induction by achieving the goal. We assume the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
can be extended from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
through a maximam
X_{l}
times and

\begin{split}X_{l}=&\frac{T_{j-l}^{*}-T_{j}^{*}}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{E(0)^{-1}(2^{-\delta_{1}(j-l)}-2^{-\delta_{1}j})}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}\\
=&\frac{C^{3}_{*}}{[E(0)]^{3}}(2^{\delta_{1}l}-1).\end{split}

(8.89)

Moreover, the following bounds

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\qquad k\geq j,\end{split}

(8.90)

and

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(8.91)

and

\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.

(8.92)

and

|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-l})\leq C_{*}.

(8.93)

In the following, we will check the estimates (
8.89
), (
8.90
), (
8.91
), (
8.92
), and (
8.93
) hold when
l=1
, and it also holds when we replace
l
by
l+1
.

Using (
8.85
), (
8.87
), (
8.82
), and (
8.88
), then (
8.90
)-(
8.93
) hold by taking
l=1
. Let us now check it for
l+1
. In this case, it implies that
T^{*}_{j-l}\leq T^{*}_{N_{0}}
. Therefore,
j-l\geq N_{0}+1
should hold. Starting at the time
T^{*}_{j-l}
, seeing (
8.21
) and (
8.93
), we shall get an extending time-interval of
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
with a length of
(C_{*})^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. Hence, we can go to the case 2 in step 1, and the length every new time-interval is
C_{*}^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}j}
. Therefore, the times is

X=\frac{T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}-T^{*}_{j-l}}{C^{-3}_{*}2^{-\delta_{1}j}}=2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}.

(8.94)

Thus, we can deduce that

X_{l+1}=X_{l}+X=(2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}.

(8.95)

Moreover, for
k\geq j
, we have

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

Using (
8.65
) and (
8.93
)

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C_{*})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3},\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(8.96)

Due to (
8.22
), it yields

\begin{split}(1+C_{*})^{3}C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}[1+E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.\end{split}

Hence, from (
8.96
) we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{j-l},T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(8.97)

Using (
8.90
) and (
8.97
), so we get

\begin{split}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{k}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\qquad k\geq j.\end{split}

(8.98)

If
k<j
, we can derive

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.99)

When we extend the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},w_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
, then the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{m},\rho_{m},w_{m})
is also extended from
[0,T^{*}_{m-l}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]
. Seeing (
8.66
) and (
8.93
), we can obtain

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(1+C_{*})^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1)C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}.\end{split}

(8.100)

Hence, for
m-l\geq N_{0}+1
, using (
8.22
), it yields

(1+C_{*})^{3}C^{3}_{*}[E(0)]^{-3}[1+E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\leq 1.

Based on the above results, (
8.100
) becomes

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[T^{*}_{m-l},T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}l}(2^{\delta_{1}}-1).\end{split}

(8.101)

Inserting (
8.91
) and (
8.101
) to (
8.99
), we have

\begin{split}&\|P_{k}(d{\rho}_{m+1}-d{\rho}_{m}),P_{k}(d{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-d{\mathbf{v}}_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)},\quad k<j,\end{split}

(8.102)

Next, we can bound

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
&+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}_{m=k}^{j-(l+2)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\Theta_{1}+\Theta_{2}+\Theta_{3}+\Theta_{4}+\Theta_{5},\end{split}

(8.103)

where

\begin{split}\Theta_{1}=&\|P_{\geq j}d\mathbf{v}_{j},P_{\geq j}d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{2}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{3}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{4}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\Theta_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.104)

On time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]
, we note that there is no growth for
\Theta_{2}
and
\Theta_{4}
in this extending process. For example, considering
\Theta_{2}
, the existing time-interval of
P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m})
is actually
[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
, and
[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]\subseteq[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]
if
m\geq j-(l+2)
. Therefore, we can use the bounds (
8.65
) and (
8.66
) to handle
\Theta_{2}
and
\Theta_{4}
. While, considering
\Theta_{1},\Theta_{3}
, and
\Theta_{5}
, we need to calculate the growth in Strichartz estimates. Based on this idea, let us give a precise analysis on (
8.104
).

According to (
8.98
), we can estimate
\Theta_{1}
by

\begin{split}\Theta_{1}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(8.105)

Due to (
8.65
), we have

\begin{split}\Theta_{2}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}},\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}.\end{split}

(8.106)

For
1\leq k\leq j-1
and
m\leq j-1
, using (
8.102
), it follows

\begin{split}\Theta_{3}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}\|P_{k}(d\mathbf{v}_{m+1}-d\mathbf{v}_{m}),P_{k}(d\rho_{m+1}-d\rho_{m})\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}.\end{split}

(8.107)

Due to (
8.65
), it yields

\begin{split}\Theta_{4}\leq&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}.\end{split}

(8.108)

If
j-l\leq k\leq j-1
, then
k+l+1-j\leq l
. By using (
8.90
), we can estimate

\begin{split}\Theta_{5}=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
=&\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}\|P_{k}d\mathbf{v}_{k},P_{k}d\rho_{k}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{k-(k+l+1-j)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}.\end{split}

(8.109)

Inserting (
8.105
), (
8.106
), (
8.107
), (
8.108
), (
8.109
) to (
8.103
), it follows

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{\infty}_{k=j}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}j}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+2)}_{m=k}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-6\delta_{1}m}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=j-(l+1)}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}m}\times 2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-(l+1)}_{k=1}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-7\delta_{1}k}\\
&+C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=j-l}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-5\delta_{1}k}2^{\delta_{1}(k+l+1-j)}\end{split}

(8.110)

In the case of
j-l\geq N_{0}+1
and
j\geq N_{0}+1
, the estimate (
8.110
) yields

\begin{split}&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{j-(l+1)}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\big\{2^{-\delta_{1}j}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}\\
&\quad+2^{-5\delta_{1}[j-(l+1)]}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1)}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}(j-l)}2^{\delta_{1}(l+1-j)}\big\}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})^{-2}\left\{2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-5\delta_{1}N_{0}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}}+2^{-6\delta_{1}N_{0}}\right\}\\
\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(8.111)

By using (
8.6
), (
8.22
), (
8.111
), and Theorem
2.10
, we have proved

\begin{split}|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}|\leq 2+C_{0},\quad E(T^{*}_{j-(l+1)})\leq C_{*}.\end{split}

(8.112)

Gathering (
8.95
), (
8.98
), (
8.102
), (
8.111
) and (
8.112
), the estimates (
8.89
)-(
8.93
) hold for
l+1
. Thus, our induction hold (
8.89
)-(
8.93
) for
l=1
to
l=j-N_{0}
. Therefore, we can extend the solutions
(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j},\mathbf{w}_{j})
from
[0,T^{*}_{j}]
to
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
when
j\geq N_{0}
. We denote

T^{*}=\min\{1,T^{*}_{N_{0}}\}=\min\{1,[E(0)]^{-3}2^{-\delta_{1}N_{0}}\}.

(8.113)

Taking
l=j-N_{0}
in (
8.92
)-(
8.93
), we therefore get

\begin{split}&E(T^{*})\leq C_{*},\quad\|\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq 2+C_{0},\\
&\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C[1+E(0)]^{3}(T^{*}_{N_{0}})^{\frac{3}{4}}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}\leq 2,\end{split}

(8.114)

where
N_{0}
and
C_{*}
(depending on
C_{0},c_{0},s,M_{0}
) are denoted in (
8.22
) and (
8.23
). Similarly, we conclude

\begin{split}\|d\mathbf{v}_{j},d\rho_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C[1+E(0)]^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(8.115)

Due to (
8.113
), (
8.114
), (
8.115
), we have proved (
8.7
), (
8.8
), and (
8.9
).

It still remains for us to prove (
8.10
) and (
8.11
). We present the proof in the following subsection.

8.3.4.
Strichartz estimates of linear wave equation on time-interval
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
.

We still expect the behaviour of a linear wave equation endowed with
g_{j}=g(\mathbf{v}_{j},\rho_{j})
. So we claim a theorem as follows

Proposition 8.3
.

For
s-\frac{3}{4}\leq r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, there is a solution
f_{j}
on
[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying the following linear wave equation

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}f_{j}=0,\\
(f_{j},\partial_{t}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(f_{0j},f_{1j}),\end{cases}

(8.116)

where
(f_{0j},f_{1j})=(P_{\leq j}f_{0},P_{\leq j}f_{1})
and
(f_{0},f_{1})\in H_{x}^{r}\times H^{r-1}_{x}
. Moreover, for
a\leq r-(s-1)
, we have

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}d{f}_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{0}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{{H}_{x}^{r-1}}),\\
&\|{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|\partial_{t}{f}_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq C_{M_{0}}(\|{f}_{0}\|_{H_{x}^{r}}+\|{f}_{1}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1}}).\end{split}

(8.117)

Proof of Proposition
8.3
.

Firstly, there are some Strichartz estimates for short time intervals. By applying the extension method described in subsection
8.3.2
and summing the short-time estimates corresponding to these intervals, we can derive a specific type of Strichartz estimate that incurs a loss of derivatives. Due to

a+9\delta_{1}\leq r-(s-1)+9\delta_{1}<r-(s-1)+(s-\frac{7}{4})<r-\frac{3}{4},

(8.118)

using (
8.116
), (
8.38
) and (
8.39
), we therefore have

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|f_{0j}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}).\end{split}

(8.119)

Next, we will discuss the cases of high frequency and low frequency. For
k\geq j
, which corresponds to the high frequency, we apply Bernstein’s inequality to obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}=&C2^{-9\delta_{1}k}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1+9\delta_{1}}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.120)

Combining (
8.119
) and (
8.120
), for
a\leq r-(s-1)
, we obtain

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{j}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j.\end{split}

(8.121)

For
k\leq j
, which corresponds to the low frequency, we need to discuss the difference terms. For any integer
m\geq 1
, we have

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m}}f_{m}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m},\partial_{t}f_{m})|_{t=0}=(f_{0m},f_{1m}),\end{cases}

and

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}f_{m+1}=0,\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m+1},\partial_{t}f_{m+1})|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)},f_{1(m+1)}).\end{cases}

Then the difference term
f_{m+1}-f_{m}
is satisfied by

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{m+1}}(f_{m+1}-f_{m})=({g}^{\alpha i}_{m+1}-{g}^{\alpha i}_{m})\partial^{2}_{\alpha i}f_{m},\quad[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
(f_{m+1}-f_{m},\partial_{t}(f_{m+1}-f_{m}))|_{t=0}=(f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m},f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}).\end{cases}

Due to (
8.39
), (
8.118
), and Duhamel’s principle, which yields

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\|f_{0(m+1)}-f_{0m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-9\delta_{1}}}+C\|f_{1(m+1)}-f_{1m}\|_{H_{x}^{r-1-9\delta_{1}}}\\
&\ +C\|({g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m})\cdot\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-9\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})+C\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\|\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}.\end{split}

(8.122)

By applying energy estimates and the Bernstein inequality, it follows that

\begin{split}\|\nabla df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|df_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{r}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r+1}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|f_{0m}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1m}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}).\end{split}

(8.123)

By utilizing the Strichartz estimates (
8.39
) and Lemma
2.7
, we obtain

\begin{split}&2^{m}\|{g}_{m+1}-{g}_{m}\|_{L^{1}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&2^{m}(T^{*}_{m+1})^{\frac{3}{4}}\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{m}(\|{\mathbf{v}}_{m+1}-{\mathbf{v}}_{m},{\rho}_{m+1}-{\rho}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\frac{3}{4}+\delta_{1}}_{x}}+\|{w}_{m+1}-{w}_{m}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}H^{\delta_{1}}_{x}})\\
\leq&C2^{-8\delta_{1}m}\left[1+E(0)\right]^{3}.\end{split}

(8.124)

Above, we also use (
8.43
), (
8.44
), and (
8.21
). According to (
8.123
) and (
8.124
), for
k<j
and
k\leq m
, (
8.122
) tells us that

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{m+1}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}m}\|(f_{0},f_{1})\|_{H^{r}_{x}\times H^{r-1}_{x}}\left[1+E(0)\right]^{3}.\end{split}

(8.125)

Although we have established some estimates for difference terms, we also need to discuss the energy estimates. This is necessary because the key point of the extension method (in subsection
8.3.2
) crucially relies on the uniform bound of the energy. To achieve this goal, we take the operator
\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}
on (
8.116
), which yields the following result:

\begin{cases}\square_{{g}_{j}}\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j}=[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j},\\
(\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j},\partial_{t}\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{j})|_{t=0}=(\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{0j},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}f_{1j}).\end{cases}

(8.126)

To estimate
[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}
, we will divide it into two cases:
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq s
and
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
.

Case 1:
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq s
.
In this situation, we note that

\begin{split}[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}=&[{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}\partial_{i}]\partial_{\alpha}f_{j}+\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(\partial_{i}g_{j}\partial_{\alpha}f_{j})\\
=&[{g}_{j}-\mathbf{m},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}\nabla]df_{j}+\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(\nabla g_{j}df_{j}).\end{split}

(8.127)

By (
8.127
) and Kato-Ponce estimates, we have
10
10

10

If
r=s
, then
L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}=L^{\infty}_{x}
.

\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+r-s}}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{s-r}}_{x}})

(8.128)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows that

\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+r-s}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(8.129)

Taking advantage of the Gagliardo-Nirenberg and Young’s inequalities, which yields

\begin{split}\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{3/4}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|^{\frac{2-s}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\end{split}

(8.130)

Case 2:
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
.
For
s<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, applying Kato-Ponce estimates, we have

\begin{split}\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}=&\|{g}^{\alpha i}_{j}-\mathbf{m}^{\alpha i},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]\partial^{2}_{\alpha i}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{2}{r-s}}_{x}}).\end{split}

(8.131)

By Sobolev’s inequality, it follows that

\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}(g_{j}-\mathbf{m})\|_{L^{\frac{2}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\leq C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}.

(8.132)

Note that
\frac{7}{4}<s\leq 2
. By applying the Gagliardo-Nirenberg and Young’s inequalities, we derive:

\begin{split}\|\nabla df_{j}\|_{L^{\frac{3}{1+s-r}}_{x}}\leq&C\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}df_{j}\|^{\frac{3/4}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{2}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|^{\frac{2-s}{3/4+(2-s)}}_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}).\end{split}

(8.133)

Therefore, in both cases, for
s-\frac{3}{4}<r\leq\frac{11}{4}
, using (
8.128
)-(
8.130
) and (
8.131
)-(
8.133
), we can derive

\begin{split}\|[\square_{{g}_{j}},\left<\nabla\right>^{r-1}]f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}\|df_{j}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

The above estimate, when combined with (
8.126
), yields

\begin{split}\frac{d}{dt}\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}})\|df_{j}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
&+C\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}.\end{split}

Using Gronwall’s inequality, we obtain

\begin{split}\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&C(\|df_{j}(0)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C\int^{t}_{0}\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|^{2}_{H^{s}_{x}}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau)\\
&\qquad\cdot\exp\left\{\int^{t}_{0}\|dg_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}+\|g_{j}-\mathbf{m}\|_{H^{s}_{x}}+\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right\}.\end{split}

(8.134)

On the other hand, the Newton-Leibniz formula tells us

\begin{split}\|f_{j}(t)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}\leq&\|f_{j}(0)\|_{L^{2}_{x}}+\int^{t}_{0}\|\partial_{t}f_{j}\|_{L^{2}_{x}}d\tau.\end{split}

(8.135)

Summarizing the results from (
8.134
), (
8.135
) and (
8.114
), for
t\in[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]
, we obtain

\begin{split}&\|f_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|df_{j}(t)\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}\\
\leq&C\textrm{e}^{C_{*}}(\|f_{0}\|^{2}_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|^{2}_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C_{*})\\
&\quad\cdot\exp\left\{\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-\frac{s}{2}-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\cdot\exp\left(\int^{t}_{0}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{r-(s-\frac{3}{4})}df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{x}}d\tau\right)\right\}.\end{split}

(8.136)

For
a\leq r-(s-1)
, based on (
8.121
), (
8.125
), and (
8.136
), and following the extension method outlined in subsection
8.3.2
, we can obtain the following bounds:

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\times\left\{2^{\delta_{1}(j-N_{0})}\right\}^{\frac{3}{4}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{3}{4}\delta_{1}N_{0}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}j}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}),\quad k\geq j,\end{split}

(8.137)

and

\begin{split}&\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-8\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})(1+E^{3}(0))\times\left\{2^{\delta_{1}(m+1-N_{0})}\right\}^{\frac{3}{4}}\\
\leq&C2^{-\frac{3}{4}\delta_{1}N_{0}}2^{-\delta_{1}k}2^{-4\delta_{1}m}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\left[1+E(0)\right]^{3},\quad k<j.\end{split}

(8.138)

By decomposing the frequency, we obtain

df_{j}=P_{\geq j}df_{j}+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{m=k}P_{k}(df_{m+1}-df_{m})+\textstyle{\sum}^{j-1}_{k=1}P_{k}df_{k}.

By using (
8.137
) and (
8.138
), we get

\begin{split}\|\left<\nabla\right>^{a-1}df_{j}\|_{L^{4}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}L^{\infty}_{x}}\leq&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\left[1+E(0)\right]^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

(8.139)

Applying (
8.136
) and (
8.139
), we obtain

\begin{split}\|f_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r}_{x}}+\|df_{j}\|_{L^{\infty}_{[0,T^{*}_{N_{0}}]}H^{r-1}_{x}}\leq&A_{*}\exp(B_{*}\mathrm{e}^{B_{*}}),\end{split}

where
C_{*}
is as stated in (
8.23
) and

\begin{split}A_{*}=&C\mathrm{e}^{C_{*}}(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}}+C_{*}),\\
B_{*}=&C(\|f_{0}\|_{H^{r}_{x}}+\|f_{1}\|_{H^{r-1}_{x}})\left[1+E(0)\right]^{3}[\frac{1}{3}(1-2^{-\delta_{1}})]^{-2}.\end{split}

At this stage, we have proved (
8.117
).
∎
